([hashgraph-online/awesome-codex-plugins](https://github.com/hashgraph-online/awesome-codex-plugins/tree/HEAD/plugins/ADanMan/clueless/skills/clueless) · ★1.6k · 5 copies)
- **clueless**: Write code at full speed while silently capturing why it was written that way, then teach it later, line by line, until the user can defend every line to a reviewer or examiner. Capture is the default on any code writing or editing task. Explanation is on request. Trigger phrases include "explain this code", "explain this line by line", "walk me through it", "help me understand what you wrote", "what does this file do", "what does this line mean", "I don't understand this code", "teach me this", "break this down", "ELI5 this code", "explain like I'm a beginner", "I have a code review tomorrow… ([Randil-Fernando/Clueless](https://github.com/Randil-Fernando/Clueless/tree/HEAD/skills/clueless) · ★1)
- **clueless-blindspots**: Use when the user asks what they are not seeing, what they forgot, what could go wrong, or what an expert would say about a plan, decision, document, code change, or setup they already have. Triggers: "what am I missing", "what don't I know", "poke holes in this", "what would an expert ask", "что я упускаю", "что я не вижу", or /clueless-blindspots. One-shot review; does not change the always-on level. ([hashgraph-online/awesome-codex-plugins](https://github.com/hashgraph-online/awesome-codex-plugins/tree/HEAD/plugins/ADanMan/clueless/skills/clueless-blindspots) · ★1.6k · 5 copies)
- **clueless-help**: Use when the user asks how clueless works, what its commands or levels are, or types /clueless-help. ([hashgraph-online/awesome-codex-plugins](https://github.com/hashgraph-online/awesome-codex-plugins/tree/HEAD/plugins/ADanMan/clueless/skills/clueless-help) · ★1.6k · 5 copies)
- **clueless-roommate**: Role-play a clueless roommate who has never taken the course — curious, forgetful, and confidently wrong — to test whether the user has truly mastered a topic. The user first gives a complete teach-back of their understanding; the roommate takes notes, builds a finite attack map, and then cross-examines the weak points one at a time. The user must explain the idea in plain language the roommate can restate, exemplify, and correct. Use when the user says "I get it — quiz me", "roommate mode", "roleplay my roommate", "prove I actually understand this", "Feynman me", or sends lecture slides/note… ([Leefandt/clueless-roommate](https://github.com/Leefandt/clueless-roommate/tree/HEAD/clueless-roommate))

- **clueproof**: Clueproof is a private photo-privacy check for UGREEN AI NAS. It reads photos the way a stranger would, with the NAS's own text recognition, vision model, face recognition and metadata, and finds the clues they give away, such as GPS location, house numbers and address labels, license plates, boarding passes and barcodes, school crests on children's clothing, ID and payment cards, keys, screens and notes with passwords, and prescription labels. A quick check answers whether new photos are safe to post and builds a ready-to-share album of the clean ones. The Stranger's File shows what a strang… ([KaraAliOsman/clueproof](https://github.com/KaraAliOsman/clueproof/tree/HEAD/skill/clueproof))
- **clues-fewshot-eval**: Evaluates few-shot learning capabilities of pre-trained language models across sentence classification, question answering, and named entity recognition tasks. It measures how well models adapt with limited labeled examples (10, 20, 30 shots) compared to fully supervised settings and human performance. Use when the user wants to benchmark on SST-2, MNLI, NER, MRC, or asks about evaluating this task. Reports macro-averaged results.
